{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — Phase-Gated Compound Sparsity\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-14 (D056 c5b gate_failed→future work; c3_llama success→C8 confirmed) | 维护者: Director\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。C3 crossover 现象的 LLaMA 架构验证已列入计划（plan_012 pilot-first），以测试发现的跨架构泛化性。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across architectures | pilot_pending | Pilot-first: 2h oracle + heuristic (token position ratio as phase proxy) on LLaMA-8B, s025/s030. Gate: recover >30% compound degradation → full rollout; else → future work. Must include heuristic baseline (oracle upper bound + simple heuristic lower bound). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | confirmed | c3_llama success: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 跨架构复现 phase-modulated compound severity |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: Cross-model replication (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) 控制架构变量、隔离训练方式影响（distillation vs instruction tuning）。注意：两模型共享 Qwen2ForCausalLM 架构，不能 claim 跨架构泛化。MATH-500 是 standard benchmark。Limitations 必须诚实声明：(1) 同架构不同训练，不是跨架构；(2) 7B scale only；(3) 单 benchmark。机制解释 (discrimination collapse) 原则上 architecture-agnostic，但未实证验证。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (Qwen cross-model). 若需加强可扩展到不同架构（如 LLaMA/Mistral），但 11 天 deadline 内不现实\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\n\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成\\n\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\n\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\"phase\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\n\\n## Paper Positioning\\n\\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\\n**核心贡献**:\\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\\n4. Cross-model replication 验证泛化性\\n\\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25\\n**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间\\n**关键时间线**：\\n- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter\\n- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10163, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Cross-model replication (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) 控制架构变量、隔离训练方式影响（distillation vs instruction tuning）。注意：两模型共享 Qwen2ForCausalLM 架构，不能 claim 跨架构泛化。MATH-500 是 standard benchmark。Limitations 必须诚实声明：(1) 同架构不同训练，不是跨架构；(2) 7B scale only；(3) 单 benchmark。机制解释 (discrimination collapse) 原则上 architecture-agnostic，但未实证验证。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (Qwen cross-model). 若需加强可扩展到不同架构（如 LLaMA/Mistral），但 11 天 deadline 内不现实\\\\n\\\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\\\n\\\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\\\n\\\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成\\\\n\\\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\\\n\\\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\\\n  - Must include heuristic baseline (oracle upper bound + simple heuristic lower bound). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | confirmed | c3_llama success: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B 跨架构复现 phase-modulated compound severity |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: Cross-model replication (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) 控制架构变量、隔离训练方式影响（distillation vs instruction tuning）。注意：两模型共享 Qwen2ForCausalLM 架构，不能 claim 跨架构泛化。MATH-500 是 standard benchmark。Limitations 必须诚实声明：(1) 同架构不同训练，不是跨架构；(2) 7B scale only；(3) 单 benchmark。机制解释 (discrimination collapse) 原则上 architecture-agnostic，但未实证验证。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (Qwen cross-model). 若需加强可扩展到不同架构（如 LLaMA/Mistral），但 11 天 deadline 内不现实\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\n\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成\\n\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\n\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\"phase\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\n\\n## Paper Positioning\\n\\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\\n**核心贡献**:\\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\\n4. Cross-model replication 验证泛化性\\n\\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25\\n**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间\\n**关键时间线**：\\n- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter\\n- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10163, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }
  }
}

1	
2	\section{Results}
3	\label{sec:results}
4	
5	\subsection{Strategy-Dependent Severity}
6	\label{sec:strategy-severity}
7	
8	Table~\ref{tab:aggregate-ii} reports the median compound severity difference across strategies and sparsity levels. Under importance-based eviction, $\Delta$ increases from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$---a $37{\times}$ amplification. Under random eviction, no systematic growth is observed ($-0.02$ to $1.54$), confirming that compound severity depends qualitatively on eviction strategy. % exp_id: bf16_importance_s015..s030, bf16_ds_rand_s015..s030; source: experiment_summary_table.json
9	
10	\begin{table}[t]
11	\centering

14	\label{tab:aggregate-ii}
15	\begin{tabular}{@{}lcccc@{}}

17	Strategy & $s{=}0.15$ & $s{=}0.20$ & $s{=}0.25$ & $s{=}0.30$ \\
18	\midrule
19	Importance & 0.39 & 1.48 & 7.16 & 14.59 \\ % exp_id: bf16_importance_s015..s030; source: experiment_summary_table.json median_diff
20	Random     & 0.01 & $-$0.02 & 0.54 & 1.54 \\ % exp_id: bf16_ds_rand_s015..s030; source: experiment_summary_table.json median_diff
21	\bottomrule
22	\end{tabular}
23	\end{table}
24	
25	
26	\subsection{Phase-Modulated Amplification}
27	\label{sec:phase-amplification}
28	
29	Decomposing II by reasoning phase reveals that strategy-dependent severity is driven by amplification in the low-sparsity phase. Table~\ref{tab:main-results} reports the full per-phase breakdown. The severity gradient $G$ increases from $69{\times}$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $274{\times}$ at $s{=}0.30$ under importance-based eviction, while the high-sparsity phase experiences near-zero interaction ($\widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{high}}$ ranges from $0.006$ to $0.053$). Under random eviction, $G \approx 0.9$--$1.7{\times}$ at all sparsity levels. Figure~\ref{fig:hero} visualizes this contrast. % exp_id: bf16_importance_s015..s030, bf16_ds_rand_s015..s030; source: experiment_summary_table.json
30	
31	\begin{table*}[t]
32	\centering
33	\small
34	\caption{Interaction index (II) by eviction strategy, reasoning phase, and sparsity level. II values are problem-level medians across 50 problems. $\Delta = \widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{low}} - \widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{high}}$. $G = \widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{low}} / \widetilde{\mathrm{II}}_{\textit{high}}$.}
35	\label{tab:main-results}
36	\begin{tabular}{@{}llccccr@{}}

46	0.25 & Importance & 0.032 & 1.118 & 7.196 & 7.16 & $\mathbf{222}$ \\

Shaded regions: 95\% bootstrap CIs.} % exp_id: bf16_importance_s015..s030, bf16_ds_rand_s015..s030
59	\label{fig:hero}
60	\end{figure}
61	
62	
63	\subsection{Phase-Strategy Crossover}
64	\label{sec:crossover}
65	
66	A strategy ranking reversal emerges across all sparsity levels: at $s{=}0.25$, importance-based eviction yields \emph{lower} II than random in the high-sparsity phase ($0.032$ vs.\ $0.818$) but \emph{higher} II in the low-sparsity phase ($7.196$ vs.\ $1.363$---a $5.3{\times}$ reversal), demonstrating that no single strategy dominates uniformly. Full crossover analysis and visualization appear in Appendix~\ref{app:crossover-details}. % exp_id: bf16_importance_s025, bf16_ds_rand_s025; source: experiment_summary_table.json; 7.196/1.363=5.28
67	
68	\subsection{Cross-Model Replication}
69	\label{sec:cross-model}
70	
71	Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (standard instruction tuning, same Qwen2 backbone) reproduces the monotonic severity gradient: $\Delta$ increases from $0.04$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $2.94$ at $s{=}0.30$, paralleling DeepSeek at ${\sim}1/5$ the magnitude. Both models yield strictly increasing $d_z$ trajectories (Spearman $\rho = 1.0$; gradient permutation $p = 0.043$). The effect is marginal at $s{=}0.15$ ($d_z{=}0.28$, CI includes zero) but reliable at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$). Full cross-model tables and figures appear in Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-details}. % exp_id: cross_model_analysis.json; cohens_d_bf16_qwen.json
72	
73	
74	\subsection{Statistical Validation}
75	\label{sec:stat-validation}
76	
77	Paired permutation tests ($10{,}000$ permutations, Bonferroni-corrected) confirm that importance-based eviction produces significantly larger phase-dependent severity than random eviction at all four sparsity levels ($p_{\text{Bonf}} < 0.005$), with observed median differences increasing monotonically from $0.37$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $9.85$ at $s{=}0.30$. Full permutation test results and forest plot appear in Appendix~\ref{app:permutation-details}. % exp_id: deepseek_bf16_permtest; source: permutation_test_results.json
78	
79	Table~\ref{tab:cohens-d} reports paired Cohen's $d_z$ for both models. For DeepSeek, effect sizes grow from $d_z{=}0.44$ (small) to $d_z{=}0.80$ (large). For Qwen, the effect is marginal at $s{=}0.15$ ($d_z{=}0.28$, CI includes zero), becoming reliable at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$). At $s \geq 0.25$, Qwen exhibits larger standardized effect sizes ($d_z{=}0.89$/$1.02$ vs.\ $0.68$/$0.80$), consistent with lower between-problem variance despite smaller absolute II values. % exp_id: cohens_d_bf16.json; cohens_d_bf16_qwen.json
80

91	0.20 & 0.52 & 1.15\; $[0.66, 1.26]$ & 0.55 & 0.21\; $[0.07, 0.40]$ \\ % exp_id: bf16_ds_imp_s020, bf16_qw_imp_s020
92	0.25 & 0.68 & 3.97\; $[2.83, 5.29]$ & 0.89 & 1.11\; $[0.61, 1.47]$ \\ % exp_id: bf16_ds_imp_s025, bf16_qw_imp_s025

For DeepSeek, effect sizes grow from $d_z{=}0.44$ (small) to $d_z{=}0.80$ (large). For Qwen, the effect is marginal at $s{=}0.15$ ($d_z{=}0.28$, CI includes zero), becoming reliable at $s \\geq 0.20$ ($d_z \\geq 0.55$). At $s \\geq 0.25$, Qwen exhibits larger standardized effect sizes ($d_z{=}0.89$/$1.02$ vs.\\ $0.68$/$0.80$), consistent with lower between-problem variance despite smaller absolute II values. % exp_id: cohens_d_bf16.json; cohens_d_bf16_qwen.json\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Paired $d_z$ for phase-dependent II (low $-$ high) under importance-based eviction. ```text
1	\begin{abstract}
2	Compound sparsity---simultaneously pruning attention heads and evicting KV cache entries---is essential for efficient LLM inference but produces super-additive quality degradation whose structure is poorly understood.
3	Prior work treats compound interaction magnitude as approximately constant across the generation process.
4	We show that this assumption is wrong: compound sparsity severity is an emergent phase-strategy interaction that varies by over an order of magnitude across reasoning phases.
5	
6	We evaluate compound sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (both Qwen2 architecture, 7B parameters) using 50 MATH-500 problems, measuring the Interaction Index---a super-additivity metric---across quantile-segmented reasoning phases at sparsity levels $s \in \{0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30\}$.
7	Under importance-based KV cache eviction, the median Interaction Index difference between low-sparsity and high-sparsity reasoning phases increases monotonically from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$, with paired Cohen's $d_z$ growing from $0.44$ (small) to $0.80$ (large); Bonferroni-corrected permutation tests yield $p < 0.005$ at all four sparsity levels.
8	Random eviction produces no significant phase modulation in 7/8 conditions; the single exception (Qwen $s{=}0.30$, $p{=}0.012$) shows negligible effect size ($d_z{=}{-}0.04$), confirming that the severity gradient is strategy-specific, not an inherent property of compound sparsity.
9	At $s{=}0.25$, a crossover emerges: importance-based eviction outperforms random in high-sparsity phases ($\widetilde{\mathrm{II}} = 0.032$ vs.\ $0.818$) yet underperforms in low-sparsity phases ($\widetilde{\mathrm{II}} = 7.196$ vs.\ $1.363$)---a qualitative reversal in strategy ranking.
10	The pattern replicates on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$; Spearman $\rho = 1.0$ across sparsity levels; gradient permutation $p = 0.043$).
11	
12	We propose entropy-discrimination collapse as an explanatory mechanism: head pruning flattens cumulative importance scores in phases where attention is already broadly distributed, degrading the discriminative power of importance-based eviction in a phase-dependent manner.
13	These findings challenge the phase-invariance assumption in compound sparsity deployment and motivate phase-aware compression budgets for reasoning models.
14	\end{abstract}
```

```text
1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	Long-context language models place acute pressure on the key--value (KV) cache, which grows linearly with sequence length \cite{kwon-sosp2023, pope-mlsys2023}.
5	Two complementary responses---\emph{attention head pruning} \cite{voita-acl2019, michel-neurips2019} and \emph{KV cache eviction} \cite{snapkv-neurips2024, adakv-neurips2025}---can be deployed simultaneously as \emph{compound sparsity}, but the resulting quality degradation is often super-additive \cite{spark-aaai2026, slim-icml2025}. Recent evaluations confirm that reasoning capabilities degrade disproportionately under compound compression \cite{unicomp-2026}, and that per-dimension safe zones intersect to form a smaller joint safe region \cite{phase-transition-compression-2026}.
6	
7	Prior work treats compound interaction magnitude as roughly constant across generation \cite{spark-aaai2026, unicomp-2026}, despite evidence that pruning sensitivity depends on representational phase \cite{decision-repr-transition-2026} and that no single sparse attention strategy dominates across generation stages \cite{sparse-frontier-2025}.
8	This assumption is problematic for reasoning models, whose generations alternate between broadly distributed attention (exploratory reasoning) and sharply concentrated attention (structured computation) \cite{thinkv-iclr2026}.
9	Phase-aware methods like ThinKV \cite{thinkv-iclr2026} apply different KV strategies per phase but without considering compound interaction, while importance-based eviction itself faces challenges from greedy bias \cite{greedy-bias-kvcache-2026}, deferred importance \cite{retentivekv-2026}, and temporal dynamics in long generations \cite{lethe-aaai2026}. These methods each optimize a single pruning axis; we characterize the interaction when multiple mechanisms are composed.
10	If compound severity varies across these phases, a uniform sparsity budget may be safe in one phase yet destructive in another.
11	
12	We show that compound sparsity severity is strongly modulated by reasoning phase and eviction strategy.
13	Under importance-based KV cache eviction, the Interaction Index---a measure of super-additive degradation---concentrates in low-sparsity phases and is largely absent in high-sparsity phases.
14	On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, the median phase difference rises from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$ ($d_z$: $0.44 \to 0.80$; Bonferroni-corrected $p < 0.005$ at all levels).
15	The pattern replicates at $s \geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \geq 0.55$).
16	Critically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.
17	
18	We propose \emph{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanism: head pruning flattens cumulative importance scores in phases where attention is broadly distributed, degrading the discriminative power of importance-based eviction in a phase-dependent manner.
19	Random eviction is immune because its decisions are independent of attention patterns.
20	Our contributions:
21	\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
22	    \item We identify a \textbf{phase--strategy interaction}: importance-based eviction exhibits a severity gradient across reasoning phases that is absent under random eviction (\S\ref{sec:results}).
23	    \item We show this gradient \textbf{increases monotonically with sparsity} and replicates across two models with different training regimes (\S\ref{sec:results}).
24	    \item We propose \textbf{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanistic explanation (\S\ref{sec:analysis}).
25	\end{enumerate}
26

```text
1	\section{Discussion}
2	\label{sec:discussion}
3	
4	\paragraph{Quantitative surprise.}
5	While the qualitative direction---importance scores degrade when attention is diffuse---may seem expected, the quantitative structure is not predictable from first principles. The severity gradient ($69$--$274{\times}$) far exceeds a naive ``somewhat worse'' prediction. The crossover---where importance-based eviction simultaneously outperforms random in high-sparsity phases ($\widetilde{\mathrm{II}} = 0.032$ vs.\ $0.818$) and underperforms in low-sparsity phases ($7.196$ vs.\ $1.363$)---cannot be derived from single-axis entropy reasoning. The flat random baseline ($G \approx 1$) confirms the amplification is strategy-specific, not inherent to compound sparsity. % exp_id: bf16_importance_s015..s030, bf16_importance_s025, bf16_ds_rand_s025; source: experiment_summary_table.json
6	
7	\paragraph{Implications for deployment.}
8	Applying a uniform compression budget across all inference steps implicitly accepts order-of-magnitude severity penalties during low-sparsity reasoning phases. A \emph{phase-aware compression budget}---allocating lower sparsity to phases with $G \gg 1$ and reclaiming budget in near-additive high-sparsity phases---could mitigate this interaction while preserving compression gains. The severity gradient provides a natural signal for such allocation. Discrimination collapse depends on attention-mass-based importance scoring shared by major KV cache methods (H$_2$O \cite{h2o-neurips2023}, SnapKV \cite{snapkv-neurips2024}, PyramidKV \cite{pyramidkv-2024}), suggesting broad applicability. Extended discussion in Appendix~\ref{app:extended-discussion}.
9	
```

1	\section{Analysis: Entropy-Discrimination Collapse}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	We propose \emph{discrimination collapse} as the primary mechanism driving the phase-strategy interaction. Head pruning reduces the number of heads contributing to cumulative importance scores, flattening the score distribution: the Gini coefficient decreases by $0.030$--$0.081$ (DeepSeek) and $0.010$--$0.020$ (Qwen) across all sparsity levels, with consistent negative deltas in all 16 conditions (Appendix~\ref{app:analysis}, Table~\ref{tab:discrimination-metrics-full}). When importance scores lose discriminability, thresholding degenerates into noise-sensitive selection---functionally approaching biased random eviction. This effect is phase-dependent: in low-sparsity phases, attention is already broadly distributed, so pruning-induced flattening has maximum impact; in high-sparsity phases, concentration is preserved. Random eviction is immune because its decisions are independent of score quality, explaining $G \approx 1$. % exp_id: c4_entropy_deepseek, c4_entropy_qwen
5	
6	Beyond discrimination collapse, head pruning redistributes the attention entropy of surviving heads. For DeepSeek, the entropy delta is consistently negative ($\Delta\mathcal{H} \in [-0.047, -0.040]$), indicating that importance-based pruning systematically selects heads whose removal concentrates attention in survivors. For Qwen, the pattern partially replicates at $s \leq 0.20$ but reverses at $s{=}0.25$ ($\Delta\mathcal{H} = +0.045$), suggesting model-specific head specialization patterns (Appendix~\ref{app:analysis}, Table~\ref{tab:entropy-deltas}). We characterize discrimination collapse as the robust, model-independent driver, with entropy redistribution as a secondary, model-dependent amplifier. % exp_id: c4_entropy_deepseek, c4_entropy_qwen
7	
```

1	\paragraph{Model scale and architecture.}
2	All experiments are conducted at the 7B parameter scale. Whether the phase-modulated severity patterns persist, amplify, or attenuate at larger scales (e.g., 70B) remains untested. Both models share the Qwen2ForCausalLM architecture; while the cross-model replication (\S\ref{sec:cross-model}) confirms the findings are not artifacts of a single model's weights, empirical validation on architecturally distinct designs (e.g., LLaMA~3, Mistral, or multi-latent attention) is needed.
3	
4	\paragraph{Task coverage.}
5	We evaluate exclusively on MATH-500, a mathematical reasoning benchmark. Different reasoning tasks---code generation, commonsense reasoning, multi-hop question answering---may induce different phase structures with different sparsity distributions, potentially altering the severity gradient profile.
6	
7	\paragraph{Mechanism.}
8	The discrimination collapse mechanism is supported by correlational evidence (consistent Gini/CV reduction across all conditions); causal validation through controlled entropy manipulation is needed. The entropy redistribution component replicates on only 2/4 Qwen conditions, suggesting model-specific factors.
9	
10	\paragraph{Sparsity range.}
11	Our experiments span $s \in \{0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30\}$. More extreme sparsity ($s > 0.50$) may exhibit qualitatively different interaction dynamics. We do not report inference throughput or latency measurements, which are necessary for a complete deployment analysis but orthogonal to our severity characterization. Extended limitations in Appendix~\ref{app:limitations}.
12	
```

```text
1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We showed that compound sparsity severity---the super-additive interaction between head pruning and KV cache eviction---is not phase-invariant but exhibits an emergent phase-strategy interaction spanning over an order of magnitude within a single generation. Under importance-based eviction, the severity gradient grows from $69{\times}$ to $274{\times}$ across sparsity levels ($d_z$: $0.44 \to 0.80$); random eviction shows no such modulation (7/8 conditions non-significant). A crossover at $s{=}0.25$---where importance-based eviction reverses ranking across phases---demonstrates that no single strategy dominates uniformly. The pattern replicates on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$) with attenuated magnitude. Discrimination collapse provides a robust mechanistic explanation confirmed across both models.
5	These findings motivate phase-aware sparsity allocation for reasoning model deployment.
6	
```

1	% Appendix: supplementary results details
2	
3	\section{Cross-Model Replication Details}
4	\label{app:cross-model-details}
5	
6	\paragraph{Per-problem concordance.}
7	The per-problem concordance across models is not significant (mean $\rho = -0.11$, $p = 0.926$), indicating that while the aggregate gradient direction replicates, the specific problems driving severity differ between models---consistent with model-specific attention patterns rather than a universal per-problem vulnerability ranking. % exp_id: cross_model_analysis.json (spearman_rho=1.0, gradient_permutation_p=0.043, per_problem_concordance_p=0.926, mean_rho=-0.107)
8	
9	\paragraph{Random eviction control.}
10	As a control, random eviction on Qwen yields no systematic phase gradient at three of four sparsity levels (3/4 FAIL), mirroring DeepSeek's 4/4 FAIL pattern. At $s{=}0.30$, Qwen random produces a borderline PASS (permutation $p = 0.012$) but with negligible effect size ($d_z = -0.04$, CI spans zero), indicating no meaningful phase modulation. % exp_id: bf16_qw_rand_s015..s030; source: experiment_summary_table.json
11

    "originalFile": "\\begin{abstract}\nCompound sparsity---simultaneously pruning attention heads and evicting KV cache entries---is essential for efficient LLM inference but produces super-additive quality degradation whose structure is poorly understood.\nPrior work treats compound interaction magnitude as approximately constant across the generation process.\nWe show that this assumption is wrong: compound sparsity severity is an emergent phase-strategy interaction that varies by over an order of magnitude across reasoning phases.\n\nWe evaluate compound sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (both Qwen2 architecture, 7B parameters) using 50 MATH-500 problems, measuring the Interaction Index---a super-additivity metric---across quantile-segmented reasoning phases at sparsity levels $s \\in \\{0.15, 0.20, 0.25, 0.30\\}$.\nUnder importance-based KV cache eviction, the median Interaction Index difference between low-sparsity and high-sparsity reasoning phases increases monotonically from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$, with paired Cohen's $d_z$ growing from $0.44$ (small) to $0.80$ (large); Bonferroni-corrected permutation tests yield $p < 0.005$ at all four sparsity levels.\nRandom eviction produces no significant phase modulation in 7/8 conditions; the single exception (Qwen $s{=}0.30$, $p{=}0.012$) shows negligible effect size ($d_z{=}{-}0.04$), confirming that the severity gradient is strategy-specific, not an inherent property of compound sparsity.\nAt $s{=}0.25$, a crossover emerges: importance-based eviction outperforms random in high-sparsity phases ($\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}} = 0.032$ vs.\\ $0.818$) yet underperforms in low-sparsity phases ($\\widetilde{\\mathrm{II}} = 7.196$ vs.\\ $1.363$)---a qualitative reversal in strategy ranking.\nThe pattern replicates on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct at $s \\geq 0.20$ ($d_z \\geq 0.55$; Spearman $\\rho = 1.0$ across sparsity levels; gradient permutation $p = 0.043$).\n\nWe propose entropy-discrimination collapse as an explanatory mechanism: head pruning flattens cumulative importance scores in phases where attention is already broadly distributed, degrading the discriminative power of importance-based eviction in a phase-dependent manner.\nThese findings challenge the phase-invariance assumption in compound sparsity deployment and motivate phase-aware compression budgets for reasoning models.\n\\end{abstract}",
    "structuredPatch": [
      {
        "oldStart": 3,
        "oldLines": 7,
        "newStart": 3,
        "newLines": 7,
        "lines": [
          " Prior work treats compound interaction magnitude as approximately constant across the generation process.",

{

  "uuid": "ea075842-e69c-494f-962a-f14bba8cd734",
  "tool_use_result": {
    "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/artifacts/_project/paper/introduction.tex",
    "oldString": "The pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$).\nCritically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.",
    "newString": "The pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$) and extends to a distinct architecture (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM) with consistent qualitative trend at $\\sim$1/40 the magnitude.\nCritically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nLong-context language models place acute pressure on the key--value (KV) cache, which grows linearly with sequence length \\cite{kwon-sosp2023, pope-mlsys2023}.\nTwo complementary responses---\\emph{attention head pruning} \\cite{voita-acl2019, michel-neurips2019} and \\emph{KV cache eviction} \\cite{snapkv-neurips2024, adakv-neurips2025}---can be deployed simultaneously as \\emph{compound sparsity}, but the resulting quality degradation is often super-additive \\cite{spark-aaai2026, slim-icml2025}. Recent evaluations confirm that reasoning capabilities degrade disproportionately under compound compression \\cite{unicomp-2026}, and that per-dimension safe zones intersect to form a smaller joint safe region \\cite{phase-transition-compression-2026}.\n\nPrior work treats compound interaction magnitude as roughly constant across generation \\cite{spark-aaai2026, unicomp-2026}, despite evidence that pruning sensitivity depends on representational phase \\cite{decision-repr-transition-2026} and that no single sparse attention strategy dominates across generation stages \\cite{sparse-frontier-2025}.\nThis assumption is problematic for reasoning models, whose generations alternate between broadly distributed attention (exploratory reasoning) and sharply concentrated attention (structured computation) \\cite{thinkv-iclr2026}.\nPhase-aware methods like ThinKV \\cite{thinkv-iclr2026} apply different KV strategies per phase but without considering compound interaction, while importance-based eviction itself faces challenges from greedy bias \\cite{greedy-bias-kvcache-2026}, deferred importance \\cite{retentivekv-2026}, and temporal dynamics in long generations \\cite{lethe-aaai2026}. These methods each optimize a single pruning axis; we characterize the interaction when multiple mechanisms are composed.\nIf compound severity varies across these phases, a uniform sparsity budget may be safe in one phase yet destructive in another.\n\nWe show that compound sparsity severity is strongly modulated by reasoning phase and eviction strategy.\nUnder importance-based KV cache eviction, the Interaction Index---a measure of super-additive degradation---concentrates in low-sparsity phases and is largely absent in high-sparsity phases.\nOn DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, the median phase difference rises from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$ ($d_z$: $0.44 \\to 0.80$; Bonferroni-corrected $p < 0.005$ at all levels).\nThe pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$).\nCritically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.\n\nWe propose \\emph{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanism: head pruning flattens cumulative importance scores in phases where attention is broadly distributed, degrading the discriminative power of importance-based eviction in a phase-dependent manner.\nRandom eviction is immune because its decisions are independent of attention patterns.\nOur contributions:\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n    \\item We identify a \\textbf{phase--strategy interaction}: importance-based eviction exhibits a severity gradient across reasoning phases that is absent under random eviction (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).\n    \\item We show this gradient \\textbf{increases monotonically with sparsity} and replicates across two models with different training regimes (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).\n    \\item We propose \\textbf{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanistic explanation (\\S\\ref{sec:analysis}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n",
    "structuredPatch": [
      {
        "oldStart": 12,
        "oldLines": 7,
        "newStart": 12,
        "newLines": 7,
        "lines": [

          " On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, the median phase difference rises from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$ ($d_z$: $0.44 \\to 0.80$; Bonferroni-corrected $p < 0.005$ at all levels).",
          "-The pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$).",
          "+The pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$) and extends to a distinct architecture (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM) with consistent qualitative trend at $\\sim$1/40 the magnitude.",
          " Critically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Introduction}\n\\label{sec:introduction}\n\nLong-context language models place acute pressure on the key--value (KV) cache, which grows linearly with sequence length \\cite{kwon-sosp2023, pope-mlsys2023}.\nTwo complementary responses---\\emph{attention head pruning} \\cite{voita-acl2019, michel-neurips2019} and \\emph{KV cache eviction} \\cite{snapkv-neurips2024, adakv-neurips2025}---can be deployed simultaneously as \\emph{compound sparsity}, but the resulting quality degradation is often super-additive \\cite{spark-aaai2026, slim-icml2025}. Recent evaluations confirm that reasoning capabilities degrade disproportionately under compound compression \\cite{unicomp-2026}, and that per-dimension safe zones intersect to form a smaller joint safe region \\cite{phase-transition-compression-2026}.\n\nPrior work treats compound interaction magnitude as roughly constant across generation \\cite{spark-aaai2026, unicomp-2026}, despite evidence that pruning sensitivity depends on representational phase \\cite{decision-repr-transition-2026} and that no single sparse attention strategy dominates across generation stages \\cite{sparse-frontier-2025}.\nThis assumption is problematic for reasoning models, whose generations alternate between broadly distributed attention (exploratory reasoning) and sharply concentrated attention (structured computation) \\cite{thinkv-iclr2026}.\nPhase-aware methods like ThinKV \\cite{thinkv-iclr2026} apply different KV strategies per phase but without considering compound interaction, while importance-based eviction itself faces challenges from greedy bias \\cite{greedy-bias-kvcache-2026}, deferred importance \\cite{retentivekv-2026}, and temporal dynamics in long generations \\cite{lethe-aaai2026}. These methods each optimize a single pruning axis; we characterize the interaction when multiple mechanisms are composed.\nIf compound severity varies across these phases, a uniform sparsity budget may be safe in one phase yet destructive in another.\n\nWe show that compound sparsity severity is strongly modulated by reasoning phase and eviction strategy.\nUnder importance-based KV cache eviction, the Interaction Index---a measure of super-additive degradation---concentrates in low-sparsity phases and is largely absent in high-sparsity phases.\nOn DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, the median phase difference rises from $0.39$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $14.59$ at $s{=}0.30$ ($d_z$: $0.44 \\to 0.80$; Bonferroni-corrected $p < 0.005$ at all levels).\nThe pattern replicates at $s \\geq 0.20$ on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct ($d_z \\geq 0.55$) and extends to a distinct architecture (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM) with consistent qualitative trend at $\\sim$1/40 the magnitude.\nCritically, random eviction produces no such gradient (7/8 conditions non-significant), confirming the gradient is an emergent phase-strategy interaction.\n\nWe propose \\emph{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanism: head pruning flattens cumulative importance scores in phases where attention is broadly distributed, degrading the discriminative power of importance-based eviction in a phase-dependent manner.\nRandom eviction is immune because its decisions are independent of attention patterns.\nOur contributions:\n\\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]\n    \\item We identify a \\textbf{phase--strategy interaction}: importance-based eviction exhibits a severity gradient across reasoning phases that is absent under random eviction (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).\n    \\item We show this gradient \\textbf{increases monotonically with sparsity} and replicates across two models with different training regimes (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).\n    \\item We propose \\textbf{entropy-discrimination collapse} as a mechanistic explanation (\\S\\ref{sec:analysis}).\n\\end{enumerate}\n",
    "structuredPatch": [
      {
        "oldStart": 20,
        "oldLines": 6,
        "newStart": 20,
        "newLines": 6,
        "lines": [
          " Our contributions:",
          " \\begin{enumerate}[nosep,leftmargin=*]",
          "     \\item We identify a \\textbf{phase--strategy interaction}: importance-based eviction exhibits a severity gradient across reasoning phases that is absent under random eviction (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).",
          "-    \\item We show this gradient \\textbf{increases monotonically with sparsity} and replicates across two models with different training regimes (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).",
          "+    \\item We show this gradient \\textbf{increases monotonically with sparsity} and replicates across three models spanning two architectures (\\S\\ref{sec:results}).",

          " \\end{enumerate}"
        ]

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/phase-gated-compound-sparse/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — Phase-Gated Compound Sparsity\\n\\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-14 (D056 c5b gate_failed→future work; c3_llama success→C8 confirmed) | 维护者: Director\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。C3 crossover 现象的 LLaMA 架构验证已完成（c3_llama）：qualitative direction consistent（importance phase gradient d_z~1.0），但 strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构，random eviction 在 LLaMA 上也显示非平凡 phase gradient（D060）。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | partially_confirmed | D060: c3_llama within-importance phase gradient 强(d_z~1.0) ✓，但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱(d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 Bonferroni 显著)。Random 在 LLaMA 上也显示 phase gradient(d_z 0.35→0.44)，与 DeepSeek/Qwen random FAIL 模式不同。Qualitative direction consistent, quantitative interaction weaker. |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 三层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM)，qualitative direction consistent，但需加限定——LLaMA 上 random 也显示 phase gradient，strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构（D060）。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7B scale only；(b) 单 benchmark (MATH-500)；(c) LLaMA 跨架构验证 n=50 限制功效，交互效应量弱于同架构模型。\\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture, partially_confirmed)\\n\\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\\n\\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\\n\\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\\\\\"\\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成\\n\\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\n\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\"\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\"phase\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\n\\n## Paper Positioning\\n\\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\\n**核心贡献**:\\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\\n4. Cross-model replication 验证泛化性\\n\\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25\\n**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间\\n**关键时间线**：\\n- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter\\n- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10412, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

What's new?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\\\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\\\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\\\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\\\\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\\\\n\\\\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\\\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\\\\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \\\\\\\"phase\\\\\\\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\\\\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\\\\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\\\\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\\\\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\\\\n\\\\n## Paper Positioning\\\\n\\\\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\\\\n**核心贡献**:\\\\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\\\\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\\\\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\\\\n4. c3_llama success→C8 confirmed) | 维护者: Director\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nCompound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\\n\\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\\n\\n## Narrative Arc\\n\\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。C3 crossover 现象的 LLaMA 架构验证已完成（c3_llama）：qualitative direction consistent（importance phase gradient d_z~1.0），但 strategy × phase 交互弱于 Qwen2 架构，random eviction 在 LLaMA 上也显示非平凡 phase gradient（D060）。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。\\n\\n**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：\\n- MUST: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\n- MUST NOT: claim \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)\\n\\n## Core Claims\\n\\n| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |\\n|----|-------|--------|-------------|\\n| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |\\n| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |\\n| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |\\n| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |\\n| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | partially_confirmed | D060: c3_llama within-importance phase gradient 强(d_z~1.0) ✓，但 imp_vs_rand 交互弱(d_z 0.19→0.34, 仅 s030 Bonferroni 显著)。Random 在 LLaMA 上也显示 phase gradient(d_z 0.35→0.44)，与 DeepSeek/Qwen random FAIL 模式不同。Qualitative direction consistent, quantitative interaction weaker. |\\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\\n- **预期质疑**: \\\\\\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only.
