```text
1	# Paper Framing — Phase-Gated Compound Sparsity
2	
3	> 最后更新: 2026-05-14 (c3_llama cross-arch→C8 confirmed; 论文纳入 LLaMA 三架构验证) | 维护者: Director
4	
5	## Core Insight
6	
7	Compound sparsity（attention head pruning + KV cache eviction）的 severity 不是固定常数——它是 eviction strategy 和 reasoning phase 交互的涌现属性。Importance-based eviction 的 compound interaction severity（paired median difference: median(II_low_i − II_high_i)）随 sparsity 单调递增（DeepSeek: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。跨架构验证（LLaMA: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM）确认 qualitative pattern 一致（imp_vs_rand d_z 0.19→0.34 单调递增, s030 p_Bonf=0.029），magnitude ~40× < DeepSeek（architecture-dependent）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。
8	
9	**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。
10	
11	## Narrative Arc
12	
13	Compound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; 唯一例外 Qwen s030 p=0.012 效应量可忽略 d=-0.04）。Cross-model replication 方向一致（Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek，onset threshold s≥0.20）。LLaMA 跨架构验证已完成（c3_llama, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B）：三架构趋势一致（imp_vs_rand d_z 单调递增, importance phase gradient d_z≈1.0），量级 architecture-dependent（LLaMA ~40× < DeepSeek）。我们通过 discrimination collapse 机制解释这一现象：head pruning → importance score 的判别力崩塌（Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致）→ importance eviction 退化为有偏伪随机。Attention entropy redistribution 作为 model-dependent secondary effect 在 DeepSeek 上观察到，但在 Qwen 上不稳健（D044）。
14	
15	**Framing 硬约束（D015）**：
16	- MUST: \\\"emergent phase-strategy interaction\\\"
17	- MUST NOT: \\\"importance metric limitation / importance is broken\\\"
18	- MUST NOT: claim \\\"importance masking produces super-additive interaction\\\" without qualifier — random masking also produces positive II. The distinguishing signal is **phase-dependent gradient** (importance: paired median difference 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random: 7/8 FAIL), not the existence of super-additivity itself (D023)
19	
20	## Core Claims
21	
22	| ID | Claim | Status | Key Evidence |
23	|----|-------|--------|-------------|
24	| C1 | Compound sparsity severity is strategy-dependent | confirmed | importance paired median diff 0.32→9.94 单调递增 vs random 7/8 FAIL (bf16; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 d=-0.04 negligible) |
25	| C2 | Severity is phase-modulated (amplified in low-sparsity phase) | confirmed | bf16 paired median difference 单调递增: 0.32→1.15→3.97→9.94; Bonferroni p<0.005 全 4 水平 (D032)。预注册：n=500 后若 QW s015 CI 仍跨零，叙事改为 'compound effect emerges above threshold sparsity (~s020)' |
26	| C3 | Phase-strategy crossover exists | confirmed | s=0.25: importance high_phase=0.032 < random=0.818, importance low_phase=7.196 > random=1.363 (bf16) |
27	| C4 | Discrimination collapse as primary mechanism; entropy redistribution model-dependent | partially_confirmed | D044: Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致; entropy collapse DeepSeek 一致但 Qwen s025 反转(+0.045) s030≈0。30/30 problems completed. |
28	| C5 | Phase-modulated compound severity generalizes across models | confirmed | DeepSeek + Qwen cross-model replication: 方向一致, Cohen's d ρ=1.0, gradient permutation p=0.043. Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |
29	| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |
30	| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |
31	| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |
32	| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | confirmed | c3_llama: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM) 跨架构复现。imp_vs_rand d_z 0.19→0.26→0.29→0.34 单调递增; s030 Wilcoxon p_Bonf=0.029; s015 median_diff CI [0.002,0.043] 不跨零; phase gradient d_z≈1.0 全水平; magnitude ~40× < DeepSeek. 三架构趋势一致，量级 architecture-dependent. |
33	| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |
34	| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |
35	| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |
36	
37	## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns
38	
39	### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark
40	- **预期质疑**: \"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\"
41	- **应对**: 三层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM) 确认跨架构泛化——qualitative pattern 一致（imp_vs_rand d_z 单调递增, phase gradient d_z≈1.0），magnitude architecture-dependent (~40× < DeepSeek)。三模型覆盖 Qwen2ForCausalLM + LlamaForCausalLM 两架构。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7-8B scale only；(b) 单 benchmark (MATH-500)；(c) 两架构族，更广泛架构验证（Mistral, MLA）仍需。
42	- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture, confirmed)
43	
44	### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量
45	- **预期质疑**: \"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\"
46	- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。
47	- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)
48	
49	### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据
50	- **预期质疑**: \"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\"
51	- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。
52	  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。
53	  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。
54	- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)
55	
56	### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够
57	- **预期质疑**: \"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\"
58	- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。
59	- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成
60	
61	### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity
62	- **预期质疑**: "ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?"
63	- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：
64	  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。
65	  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。
66	  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。
67	  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。
68	- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析
69	
70	### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK
71	- **预期质疑**: "How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?"
72	- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：
73	  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 "phase" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。
74	  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。
75	  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。
76	  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。
77	- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分
78	
79	## Paper Positioning
80	
81	**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）
82	**核心贡献**:
83	1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互
84	2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象
85	3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）
86	4. Cross-model + cross-architecture (LLaMA) replication 验证泛化性（三模型、两架构）
87	
88	**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25
89	**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间
90	**关键时间线**：
91	- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter
92	- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿
93

Qwen magnitude ~1/4 of DeepSeek, onset threshold s≥0.20 (S3→C). |\n| C5b | Phase-aware mitigation recovers compound degradation | future_work | D056: pilot 完成 gate_failed。Oracle recovery 18% < 30% gate (Wilcoxon p=0.002)，heuristic 32% 但 CI 跨零。方向正确但效应量不足，需更精细 phase boundary estimation → future work |\n| C6 | Severity gradient is sub-exponential | confirmed | Growth rate decreasing: Δ增长因子 3.8×→4.8×→2.0×（递减） |\n| C7 | Statistical power at n=500 confirms dose-response | running | c2_n500_deepseek + c2_qw_n500 (n=500, 8 conditions each) |\n| C8 | Cross-architecture validation (LLaMA) | confirmed | c3_llama: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM) 跨架构复现。imp_vs_rand d_z 0.19→0.26→0.29→0.34 单调递增; s030 Wilcoxon p_Bonf=0.029; s015 median_diff CI [0.002,0.043] 不跨零; phase gradient d_z≈1.0 全水平; magnitude ~40× < DeepSeek. 三架构趋势一致，量级 architecture-dependent. |\n| C9 | Differentiation from Lethe/SparK prior work | planned | Related Work §2 引用区分 + SparK gap analysis |\n| S3→C | Onset threshold is model-dependent (DeepSeek s≥0.15, Qwen s≥0.20) | confirmed | D037/D038: Qwen s015 d=0.28 CI含0, s020+ 显著 |\n| S7→C | Dose-response monotonicity (Spearman ρ=1.0) | confirmed | D047: 6-point dose-response, 单调递增 |\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n### RC1: Generalizability — 7B models only, single benchmark\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"Results on two 7B models and MATH-500 only. How do we know this generalizes to larger models or other reasoning tasks?\\\"\n- **应对**: 三层泛化证据：(1) Cross-model: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B + Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（同架构不同训练，ρ=1.0）(2) Cross-architecture: c3_llama (DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM) 确认跨架构泛化——qualitative pattern 一致（imp_vs_rand d_z 单调递增, phase gradient d_z≈1.0），magnitude architecture-dependent (~40× < DeepSeek)。三模型覆盖 Qwen2ForCausalLM + LlamaForCausalLM 两架构。Limitations 诚实声明：(a) 7-8B scale only；(b) 单 benchmark (MATH-500)；(c) 两架构族，更广泛架构验证（Mistral, MLA）仍需。\n- **实验支撑**: C5 (cross-model) + C8 (c3_llama cross-architecture, confirmed)\n\n### RC2: Mechanism — entropy-discrimination collapse 缺乏直接测量\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"The mechanism is conceptual. Where is the empirical evidence for entropy increase and discrimination collapse?\\\"\n- **应对**: c4_entropy 实验已完成（DeepSeek 30/30 + Qwen 30/30）。D044 结论：Gini/CV 下降跨模型一致（discrimination collapse confirmed）；entropy redistribution 在 DeepSeek 上一致但在 Qwen 上 s025 方向反转、s030 近零（model-dependent）。论文以 discrimination collapse 为主线，entropy 作 DeepSeek 特有的强化因素。\n- **实验支撑**: c4_entropy (DeepSeek DONE) + qwen_c4_entropy (DONE, negative for entropy universality)\n\n### RC3: Practical impact — 没有 throughput 数据\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"No wall-clock or throughput measurements. Is the severity amplification reflected in actual inference speed?\\\"\n- **应对**: 本文定位是 empirical finding paper（发现 + 机制解释），不是 method paper。Throughput 不是 claim 的一部分。Discussion 中明确 phase-gated controller 作为 future direction。\n  - C5b pilot 已完成（D056）：oracle recovery 18%（Wilcoxon p=0.002），heuristic recovery 32%（p=0.024），但两者 95% CI 均跨零。方向正确（Wilcoxon 显著）但效应量不足（未达 30% gate），需更精细的 phase boundary estimation。论文 §6 Discussion 诚实报告 pilot 结果 + future work。\n  - Heuristic > oracle 悖论值得提及：token position ratio 粗粒度划分可能恰好更适合 mitigation 场景，暗示 phase boundary 的最优粒度是开放问题。\n- **实验支撑**: c5b_pilot (gate_failed, D056)\n\n### RC4: Statistical rigor — bootstrap 是否足够\n- **预期质疑**: \\\"Per-problem bootstrap with 50 problems — sufficient sample size?\\\"\n- **应对**: bf16 使用 B=10000 per-problem paired bootstrap，有效配对数 n=49 (DeepSeek) / n=46 (Qwen)。Permutation test 使用 paired median difference 作为 robust statistic（D032），Bonferroni 校正后全 4 水平 p<0.005。Cohen's d（paired d_z）作为辅助效应量（DeepSeek 0.44→0.80, Qwen 0.28→1.02）。可在 appendix 补充 per-problem 分布图（已生成 fig4_per_problem_dist.pdf）。\n- **实验支撑**: bf16 permutation test (D032) + Cohen's d per-problem paired (rebootstrap B=10000) 已完成\n\n### RC5: Novelty — prior work 是否已覆盖 phase-dependent sparsity\n- **预期质疑**: \"ThinKV and phase-aware methods already consider phase. What's new?\"\n- **应对**: Prior work 在单轴上分析 phase（ThinKV: phase-aware eviction）或 compound（SparK: joint compression）。没有工作同时分析 compound × phase × strategy 的三维交互。Crossover 现象（同一 strategy 在不同 phase 方向反转）是全新发现。Related Work 加 Lethe 引用区分 + SparK 对比。具体区分：\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025): 在 KV cache 单轴上做 phase-aware 压缩（按 CoT 阶段差异化 eviction），但不涉及 pruning × eviction 的 compound interaction。我们研究的是双轴（head pruning × KV eviction）compound interaction 如何随 phase 变化，ThinKV 的单轴 phase-aware 策略是我们分析维度的子集。\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\n\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\n- **预期质疑**: \"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\"\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \"phase\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\n\n## Paper Positioning\n\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\n**核心贡献**:\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\n4. Cross-model + cross-architecture (LLaMA) replication 验证泛化性（三模型、两架构）\n\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25\n**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间\n**关键时间线**：\n- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter\n- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿\n",

Qwen: 0.048→0.208→1.109→2.619），而 random masking 三相 II 持平（7/8 FAIL; Qwen s030 PASS p=0.012 but d=-0.04 negligible）。Cohen's d（paired d_z, B=10000）从 small-medium 到 large（DeepSeek: 0.44→0.80; Qwen: 0.28→1.02，s015 边缘）。跨架构验证（LLaMA: DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B, LlamaForCausalLM）确认 qualitative pattern 一致（imp_vs_rand d_z 0.19→0.34 单调递增, s030 p_Bonf=0.029），magnitude ~40× < DeepSeek（architecture-dependent）。这一发现挑战了 compound degradation 均匀分布的隐含假设。\n\n**数字基准**：所有数字基于 bf16 per-problem paired 统计量（B=10000 bootstrap）。median_diff 定义为 median(II_low_i − II_high_i)（逐 problem 配对差的中位数）。fp16 旧数据已弃用。\n\n## Narrative Arc\n\nCompound sparsity 是高效 LLM 推理的实用部署需求，但 prior work（SparK, Ada-KV, SLiM）将 compound interaction 视为均匀超可加。我们发现 severity 是 strategy × phase 交互的涌现属性：importance eviction 的 compound interaction 在 low-sparsity phase 远大于 high-sparsity phase（paired median difference 单调递增 0.32→9.94），而 random eviction 在所有 phase 保持平坦（7/8 FAIL; **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026): 揭示了单轴 KV 压缩的 phase transition 和 representational rigidity，是机制层面最接近的工作。关键区分：他们刻画的是单一压缩轴上压缩率的 critical threshold（phase transition），我们刻画的是两种压缩方法的 compound interaction severity 如何被 reasoning phase 调制（phase-dependent gradient）——维度正交。\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025): 发现 importance score collapse 并提出 entropy 补偿，为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供独立 supporting evidence——他们在单轴上观察到的 score collapse 正是我们在 compound setting 中发现的 Gini/CV 下降的单轴对应。\n  - **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025): 针对 reasoning task 做 KV compression 优化，属于 task-specific method。我们不是提出新方法，而是 mechanistic 分析 compound interaction 的 phase-dependent 行为，二者目标不同（optimization vs understanding）。\n- **实验支撑**: C3 (crossover), Related Work §2 的 gap 分析\n\n### RC6: Novelty vs Lethe/SparK\n- **预期质疑**: \"How does this differ from Lethe (AAAI 2026) which also does layer-time-adaptive KV pruning, or SparK which studies compound compression?\"\n- **应对**: Lethe 和 SparK 都没有分析 phase × strategy 的交互效应。Lethe 是 method paper（提出 adaptive eviction 方法），我们是 empirical finding paper（发现 phase-dependent severity gradient）。SparK 研究 compound compression 但将 interaction 视为均匀超可加，我们发现 interaction severity 随 phase 和 strategy 变化。新近工作进一步确认我们的独特定位：\n  - **Physics of KV Cache Compression** (arxiv 2603.01426, Mar 2026) 是最近的相关工作，研究单轴压缩的 phase transition 阈值行为。与我们的核心区别：他们的 phase transition 是压缩率维度上的 critical point，我们的 phase-dependent gradient 是 reasoning phase 维度上 compound interaction 的连续调制——两者刻画的 \"phase\" 语义不同（compression-rate phase vs reasoning phase）。\n  - **ThinKV** (arxiv 2510.01290, Oct 2025) 和 **R-KV** (arxiv 2505.24133, May 2025) 都在 KV compression 单轴上引入 phase/reasoning awareness，但均未涉及 compound interaction。我们的贡献在于揭示 pruning × eviction 双轴交互如何被 reasoning phase 调制，这是单轴方法无法观察到的涌现现象。\n  - **HIES** (arxiv 2510.13832, Oct 2025) 的 importance score collapse 发现为我们的 discrimination collapse 机制提供了独立佐证，但 HIES 聚焦于单轴补偿方法，未研究 collapse 在 compound setting 中的放大效应。\n  - Related Work §2 明确区分三者定位：Lethe/SparK = method/compound method, ThinKV/R-KV = single-axis phase-aware, Physics of KV = single-axis phase transition, HIES = single-axis collapse。我们 = compound × phase × strategy 三维交互分析。\n- **实验支撑**: 不需要额外实验，仅需 §2 引用区分\n\n## Paper Positioning\n\n**类型**: Empirical finding paper（非 method paper）\n**核心贡献**:\n1. 发现 compound sparsity severity 的 strategy × phase 交互\n2. 量化 severity gradient（bf16 paired median difference 0.32→9.94, Cohen's d 0.44→0.80）和 crossover 现象\n3. 提出 discrimination collapse 机制解释（Gini/CV 跨模型一致；entropy redistribution model-dependent）\n4. Cross-model + cross-architecture (LLaMA) replication 验证泛化性（三模型、两架构）\n\n**投稿目标**: EMNLP 2026 ARR, deadline 2026-05-25\n**Rebuttal 准备**: Day 9 (2026-05-23) 预留 rebuttal letter 写作时间\n**关键时间线**：\n- Day 9 (2026-05-23): 预留写 rebuttal letter\n- Day 11 (2026-05-25): EMNLP 2026 ARR 截稿\n"
}

68	\subsection{Cross-Model Replication}
69	\label{sec:cross-model}
70	
71	Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (standard instruction tuning, same Qwen2 backbone) reproduces the monotonic severity gradient: $\Delta$ increases from $0.04$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $2.94$ at $s{=}0.30$, paralleling DeepSeek at ${\sim}1/5$ the magnitude. Both models yield strictly increasing $d_z$ trajectories (Spearman $\rho = 1.0$; gradient permutation $p = 0.043$). The effect is marginal at $s{=}0.15$ ($d_z{=}0.28$, CI includes zero) but reliable at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$). Full cross-model tables and figures appear in Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-details}. % exp_id: cross_model_analysis.json; cohens_d_bf16_qwen.json
72	
73	
74	\subsection{Cross-Architecture Validation}
75	\label{sec:cross-arch}
76	
77	To test whether the phase-modulated severity pattern extends beyond the Qwen2 architecture, we replicate the experiment on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B (LlamaForCausalLM, 8B parameters; $n{=}49$ paired problems). Table~\ref{tab:llama-cross-arch} reports the strategy-dependent severity comparison across sparsity levels.
78	
79	The qualitative pattern replicates: importance-based eviction produces systematically larger phase-dependent severity than random eviction, with monotonically increasing paired $d_z$ ($0.19 \to 0.26 \to 0.29 \to 0.34$). At $s{=}0.30$, the Wilcoxon test reaches significance after Bonferroni correction ($p = 0.029$, $k{=}4$). At $s{=}0.15$, the median difference CI $[0.002, 0.043]$ excludes zero despite the small absolute magnitude ($\widetilde{\Delta} = 0.020$). Under importance-based eviction, the within-strategy phase gradient $d_z$ (low $-$ high) is consistently large at all sparsity levels ($d_z \in [1.00, 1.12]$), while random eviction yields only small phase gradients ($d_z \in [0.35, 0.45]$). % exp_id: c3_llama
80	
81	The absolute magnitude is architecture-dependent: LLaMA severity differences are ${\sim}40{\times}$ smaller than DeepSeek and ${\sim}7{\times}$ smaller than Qwen, but the directional pattern---monotonically increasing $d_z$, importance $>$ random, large phase gradient under importance---is consistent across all three models spanning two architectures (Qwen2ForCausalLM, LlamaForCausalLM). % exp_id: c3_llama; cross_model_analysis.json
82

102	\label{sec:stat-validation}
103	
104	Paired permutation tests ($10{,}000$ permutations, Bonferroni-corrected) confirm that importance-based eviction produces significantly larger phase-dependent severity than random eviction at all four sparsity levels ($p_{\text{Bonf}} < 0.005$), with observed median differences increasing monotonically from $0.37$ at $s{=}0.15$ to $9.85$ at $s{=}0.30$. Full permutation test results and forest plot appear in Appendix~\ref{app:permutation-details}. % exp_id: deepseek_bf16_permtest; source: permutation_test_results.json
105	
106	Table~\ref{tab:cohens-d} reports paired Cohen's $d_z$ for both models. For DeepSeek, effect sizes grow from $d_z{=}0.44$ (small) to $d_z{=}0.80$ (large). For Qwen, the effect is marginal at $s{=}0.15$ ($d_z{=}0.28$, CI includes zero), becoming reliable at $s \geq 0.20$ ($d_z \geq 0.55$). At $s \geq 0.25$, Qwen exhibits larger standardized effect sizes ($d_z{=}0.89$/$1.02$ vs.\ $0.68$/$0.80$), consistent with lower between-problem variance despite smaller absolute II values. % exp_id: cohens_d_bf16.json; cohens_d_bf16_qwen.json
